# rust/lance-file/test_data/exact_versions/v2_1.lance

**Analyzed binary: all 501,167 bytes; line_count=null.** Intervals are zero-based and half-open. Pinned standard V2_1 binary fixture: 4,097 rows, five physical columns, 25 pages and 73 mini-blocks. All payloads, FastLanes packed bits and zero tails, repetition/definition levels, indices, offsets, dictionary keys, blob descriptions, schema/metadata tags, alignment and footer bytes are interpreted. No callable symbols.

[Module contract](../../../../../L1-modules/archive/rust-lance-file-standard-v21-fixture.md) · [Format specification](../../../../../L3-symbols/format-spec.md) · [Behavior contracts](../../../../../L3-symbols/behavior-contracts.md) · [Cookbook](../../../../../L4-crosscutting/cookbook.md) · [Coverage ledger](../../../../../archive/pre-v21/MANIFEST.json)

SHA-256: `3af97ba176b72c7e00a248b4a270a53402a72e594631950f76eb3daab45c50ce`. Offline KB-side interpretation only. No Lance runtime, build or generator was executed. All six lance-file fixture binaries are fully interpreted; complete encoding-crate analysis remains pending. This analysis does not independently qualify the historical baseline provenance.

[Preserved original bytes](../../../../../evidence/binary/v2_1.lance), [all decoded rows](../../../../../evidence/binary/v2_1-decoded-rows.json), [full page/chunk/level/wire catalog](../../../../../analysis/iteration-069-v2_1-fixture.json), [semantic decoder](../../../../../tools/analyze-standard-v21-fixture.mjs), [independent expected-byte assembler](../../../../../tools/verify-standard-v21-reconstruction.mjs).

## Whole-file semantics

For rows r=0..4096: id=r; name=null when r%7=0, otherwise value-NNNN-deterministic-fixture (32 ASCII bytes); items=null when r%11=0, otherwise [r,(null if r%5=0 else 2*r),3*r]; category=null when r%13=0, otherwise [red,green,blue][r%3]; blob is always blob-NNNN-deterministic-payload (31 ASCII bytes). NNNN is r padded to four decimal digits. Null counts: name 586, list 373, child 745, category 316; 11,172 physical children and 11,545 list level entries. No empty lists or null/empty blobs occur. (rust/lance-file/test_data/exact_versions/datagen.rs:37-99; rust/lance-file/test_data/exact_versions/v2_1.lance@bytes:0-501167)

Five batches contain 1024/1024/1024/1024/1 rows. Each writes out-of-line blobs first, then one page per physical column in id/name/items/category/blob order. Blob bases are 0,123648,247936,372608,497408; every 31-byte payload has 33 H (0x48) alignment bytes. Page buffers likewise align separately to 64 bytes with H padding; metadata sizes exclude padding. Mini-block components align to 8 with 0xFE. Data region [0,498240) consists of 233414 page-buffer bytes, 2618 page-buffer padding bytes, 127007 blob bytes and 135201 blob padding bytes. Page-buffer bytes include the mini-block internal headers/padding, dictionary blocks and repetition indices. (rust/lance-file/test_data/exact_versions/datagen.rs:102-131; rust/lance-encoding/src/encoder.rs:125-165; rust/lance-encoding/src/encodings/logical/primitive.rs:5751-5910; rust/lance-file/test_data/exact_versions/v2_1.lance@bytes:0-498240)

The unframed descriptor [498240,498567) has six flattened field IDs but only five physical columns: parent items and child item share column2. Column metadata [498567,501031), CMO [501031,501111), GBO [501111,501127), footer [501127,501167). Footer is A498567,B501031,C501111,global_count1,column_count5,major2,minor1,LANC. Every column uses ColumnEncoding Values; page Any type is /lance.encodings21.PageLayout. Blob wrapping occurs in PageLayout. Priorities are global batch row starts 0/1024/2048/3072/4096. (protos/file2.proto:40-93; protos/file2.proto:138-216; protos/encodings_v2_1.proto:288-358; rust/lance-file/test_data/exact_versions/v2_1.lance@bytes:498240-501167)

## Complete mini-block catalog

Packed word low four bits give log2(values), except 0 means the remaining page values. A full final chunk may keep a nonzero log (all full blob chunks use 8); do not require a sentinel on the final chunk. High 12 bits store chunk_bytes/8-1. Headers contain UInt16 num_levels, optional rep_size, optional def_size and one value-buffer size, all little-endian. Each component then pads to 8 bytes. Chunk values count visible physical slots, including null child backing; level counts also include invisible null-list markers. ## Every byte region

### Bytes 0-65536

Batch0 rows[0,1024): 1024 records of64 bytes; each contains31 ASCII bytes blob-NNNN-deterministic-payload followed by33 bytes0x48. NNNN is the global row number padded to4 decimal digits. No prefix or compression; descriptions store position=0+64*local_row,size31.

Evidence: rust/lance-file/test_data/exact_versions/v2_1.lance@bytes:0-65536. Interpretation: rust/lance-encoding/src/encoder.rs:125-165; rust/lance-encoding/src/encodings/logical/blob.rs:105-203. SHA-256: `1b7e127820f99e28dd148886da54489473de3237dcd392079c431a5e8c258217`.
